``Expect.*`` assertion primitives (COMP-010).\n\nRoadmap COMP-010 / FR-EXP1 / Deliverable D-0064 (Task T04.01).\n\nSeven primitives — :meth:`Expect.file`, :meth:`Expect.jsonl`,\n:meth:`Expect.settings_json`, :meth:`Expect.exit_code`,\n:meth:`Expect.stderr`, :meth:`Expect.stdout`, :meth:`Expect.duration` —\neach return an :data:`ExpectCallable` (DM-009) The returned callable\n  closes over the configured arguments and executes at runtime.\n* **Declarative** — manifest authors write a YAML mapping like\n  ``{exit_code: {equals: 0}}`` and :meth:`Expect.from_mapping` resolves\n  the key against the primitive registry. \"\"\"\n\n    p = Path(path)\n    if p.is_absolute():\n        return p\n    return ctx.home_path / p\n\n\ndef _strip_ansi(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return ``text`` with ANSI escape sequences :meth:`Expect.stdout` /\n    :meth:`Expect.stderr` still get clean predicate matches.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    return ANSI_ESCAPE_RE.sub(\"\", text)\n\n\ndef _make_failure(\n    ctx: EvalContext,\n    *,\n difflib.unified_diff(\n        expected.splitlines(keepends=True),\n        actual.splitlines(keepends=True),\n        fromfile=f\"expected/{name}\",\n        tofile=f\"actual/{name}\",\n JSONL log records it.\"\"\"\n\n    fn.__name__ = name\n    return fn\n\n\ndef _timed_result(\n    name: str,\n    builder: Callable[[], tuple[bool, str, Mapping[str, Any], Optional[ExpectFailure]]],\n) -> ExpectResult:\n    \"\"\"Run ``builder`` and wrap the outcome in an ExpectResult with timing.\"\"\"\n\n    start = time.monotonic()\n    passed, message, details, failure = builder()\n    duration = time.monotonic() - start\n    return ExpectResult(\n        name=name,\n        passed=passed,\n        message=message,\n        details=details,\n        duration_sec=duration,\n        failure=failure,\n    )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Public DSL surface\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass Expect:\n    \"\"\"COMP-010 / FR-EXP1 assertion primitive namespace.\n\n    The class itself is never instantiated; each primitive is a\n    ``@staticmethod`` returning an :data:`ExpectCallable`. This mirrors\n    the M1 stub interface (T01.14) so manifest authors who shaped their\n    ``expects:`` blocks against the stubs find a drop-in replacement.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # ---- COMP-010.1 -------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def file(\n        *,\n        path: str | Path,\n        exists: Optional[bool] = None,\n        contains: Optional[str] = None,\n        regex: Optional[str] = None,\n        equals: Optional[str] = None,\n    ) -> ExpectCallable:\n        \"\"\"Assert a file's existence and / or content (COMP-010.1).\n\n        ``path`` resolves against :attr:`EvalContext.home_path` when\n        relative. At least one of ``exists`` / ``contains`` / ``regex`` /\n        ``equals`` should be set; when all are ``None`` the primitive\n        only asserts the file is readable.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        compiled = re.compile(regex) if regex is not None else None\n\n        def _run(ctx: EvalContext) -> ExpectResult:\n            def _build() -> tuple[bool, str, Mapping[str, Any], Optional[ExpectFailure]]:\n                resolved = _resolve_path(ctx, path)\n                file_exists = resolved.exists()\n\n                if exists is not None and file_exists != bool(exists):\n                    msg = f\"file existence: expected {bool(exists)} got {file_exists}\"\n                    return False, msg, {\"path\": str(resolved), \"exists\": file_exists}, _make_failure(\n                        ctx,\n                        name=\"file\",\n                        expected={\"exists\": bool(exists)},\n                        actual={\"exists\": file_exists},\n                        message=msg,\n                    )\n\n                if not file_exists:\n                    # Nothing else to assert against a missing file.\n                    return True, \"file absent (per exists=False)\", {\"path\": str(resolved)}, None\n\n                content = resolved.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n\n                if equals is not None and content != equals:\n                    diff = _unified_diff(equals, content, name=str(resolved))\n                    return False, \"file content does not equal expected\", {\n                        \"path\": str(resolved),\n                        \"diff\": diff,\n                    }, _make_failure(\n                        ctx,\n                        name=\"file\",\n                        expected=equals,\n                        actual=content,\n                        message=\"file content does not equal expected\",\n                    )\n\n                if contains is not None and contains not in content:\n                    return False, f\"file missing substring {contains!r}\", {\n                        \"path\": str(resolved),\n                    }, _make_failure(\n                        ctx,\n                        name=\"file\",\n                        expected={\"contains\": contains},\n                        actual=content,\n                        message=f\"substring {contains!r} not found in {resolved}\",\n                    )\n\n                if compiled is not None and compiled.search(content) is None:\n                    return False, f\"file does not match regex {regex!r}\", {\n                        \"path\": str(resolved),\n                    }, _make_failure(\n                        ctx,\n                        name=\"file\",\n                        expected={\"regex\": regex},\n                        actual=content,\n                        message=f\"regex {regex!r} did not match {resolved}\",\n                    )\n\n                return True, \"file assertions satisfied\", {\"path\": str(resolved)}, None\n\n            return _timed_result(\"file\", _build)\n\n        return _named_callable(\"file\", _run)\n\n    # ---- COMP-010.2 -------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def jsonl(\n        *,\n        path: str | Path,\n        line_count: Optional[int] = None,\n        filter: Optional[Callable[[Mapping[str, Any]], bool]] = None,\n        assert_each: Optional[Callable[[Mapping[str, Any]], bool]] = None,\n        assert_any: Optional[Callable[[Mapping[str, Any]], bool]] = None,\n    ) -> ExpectCallable:\n        \"\"\"Assert JSONL entries match a predicate (COMP-010.2).\n\n        ``path`` first resolves def _run(ctx: EvalContext) -> ExpectResult:\n            def _build() -> tuple[bool, str, Mapping[str, Any], Optional[ExpectFailure]]:\n                path_str = str(path)\n                if @staticmethod\n    def exit_code(\n        equals: Any = _SENTINEL,\n        in_set: Optional[Iterable[int]] = None,\n        not_equals: Optional[int] = None,\n    ) -> ExpectCallable:\n        \"\"\"Assert the subprocess exit code (COMP-010.4).\n\n        Default behaviour asserts ``equals=0``. ``equals`` and ``in_set``\n        are mutually exclusive — supplying both raises ``ValueError``     )\n\n        normalized_set: Optional[frozenset[int]] = (\n            frozenset(in_set) if in_set is not None else None\n        )\n\n        def _run(ctx: EvalContext) -> ExpectResult:\n                ctx,\n                            name=\"exit_code\",\n                            expected={\"in_set\": sorted(normalized_set)},\n                            actual=code,\n Returns an\n`ExpectCallable = Callable[[EvalContext], ExpectResult]` (DM-009).\n\n## Argument semantics\n\n| Arg | Type | When set | Pass condition |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `path` | `str | Path` | Always required | Resolves relative paths against `ctx.home_path` (DM-006); absolute paths used verbatim. |\n| `exists` | `Optional[bool]` | Existence check | Filesystem `Path.exists()` matches the expected boolean. If `exists=False` and the file is absent, downstream content checks are short-circuited (PASS). |\n| `contains` | `Optional[str]` | Substring check | The literal substring appears in the UTF-8 decoded body. |\n| `regex` | `Optional[str]` | Pattern check | `re.compile(regex).search(content)` returns a match. Compiled once at primitive construction. |\n| `equals` | `Optional[str]` | Exact-content check | UTF-8 body equals the expected string. Failure carries a unified diff in `details[\"diff\"]`. |\n\nEvaluation order inside the primitive:\n\n1. `exists` (existence delta)\n2. early return PASS when `exists=False` and file is absent\n3. read body as UTF-8 with `errors=\"replace\"`\n4. `equals` (exact match) → unified diff on failure\n5. `contains` (substring)\n6. `regex` (compiled-pattern search)\n\nThe first failing check produces the `ExpectResult.failure` (DM-005); the\nremaining checks are not evaluated.\n\n## Diff format (AC contract)\n\nOn `equals` mismatch, the failure ExpectResult populates two surfaces:\n\n* `result.details[\"diff\"]` — unified-diff string produced by\n  `difflib.unified_diff` with:\n  * `fromfile = f\"expected/{resolved_path}\"`\n  * `tofile   = f\"actual/{resolved_path}\"`\n  * context = 3 lines (default)\n* `result.failure.expected` / `result.failure.actual` — the raw expected\n  string and the raw file body so a Reporter can re-render the diff at a\n  different context-width.\n\nExample failure diff (from `test_equals_fails_with_unified_diff_in_details`):\n\n```diff\n--- expected/.../x.txt\n+++ actual/.../x.txt\n@@ -1,3 +1,3 @@\n line one\n-LINE TWO\n+line two\n line three\n```\n\n## Failure payload (other branches)\n\n* `exists` mismatch → `expected={\"exists\": <bool>}`, `actual={\"exists\": <bool>}`.\n* `contains` miss → `expected={\"contains\": <substr>}`, `actual=<full body>`.\n* `regex` miss → `expected={\"regex\": <pattern>}`, `actual=<full body>`.\n\nThis keeps the Reporter (T03.13) and Eval JSONL log (T03.05) free to\nrender content-aware diffs even for non-`equals` failures, without the\nprimitive paying the diff cost on every assertion.\n\n## Path resolution invariants\n\n* Absolute `path` arguments bypass `home_path` entirely.\n* Relative `path` arguments resolve via `home_path / path` (DM-006).\n* Resolution is performed at *callable invocation time*, not construction\n  time, so a manifest authored once can run against multiple per-eval\n  HOMEs.\n\n## Test matrix (`tests/cli/eval/test_expect_file.py`)\n\n| # | Test | Argument under test | Direction |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | No body changes were required to\n`src/superclaude/cli/eval/expect.py`.\n\n## Decisions\n\n1. **Unified diff lives in `details[\"diff\"]`, not the message.** The\n   M1 stubs put compact reasons in `result.message` for the console\n   reporter; a multiline diff there would smear the one-line summary.\n   Keeping the diff in `details` lets the Reporter (T03.13) render it in\n   the verbose section without re-parsing.\n2. **`exists=False` short-circuits content checks.** When a manifest\n   asserts a side-effect file should *not* be present, the primitive must\n   not then attempt to read the (absent) file and crash. The existing\n   implementation already had this; the test\n   (`test_exists_false_passes_when_missing`) pins the contract.\n3. **`regex` uses `re.search`, not `re.match`.** Manifest authors expect\n   \"does the pattern appear anywhere in the file\" semantics; the test\n   `test_regex_matches_across_lines_via_search` documents this.\n4. **Path resolution at call time.** The closure resolves\n   `ctx.home_path / path` inside `_run`, not at primitive construction,\n   so one manifest-built callable runs against many EvalContexts (one per\n   eval invocation).\n\n## Things explicitly not covered here\n\n* `regex` invalid-pattern handling — `re.compile` raising on bad regex\n  is intentional fail-fast at primitive construction; covered by the\n  declarative loader tests in T01.14.\n* Binary-file behaviour — the primitive decodes UTF-8 with\n  `errors=\"replace\"`, so binary fixtures degrade gracefully but should\n  not be the test target. Manifest authors expecting binary content\n  comparison must use a custom helper.\n* `equals` whitespace normalisation — none performed; `equals` is strict\n  byte-for-byte (after UTF-8 decode). This matches the FR-EXP1 spec.\n\n## Follow-ups\n\nNone. Two adjacent tasks consume this primitive:\n\n* T05.02 (E1) wires `Expect.file` into the sticky-lifecycle eval.\n* T05.10 (E9) wires it into per-matcher artifact assertions for the\n  coverage gate.\n\nBoth pass without any further changes to `Expect.file`.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":275,"cache_read_input_tokens":125856,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":275,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":58,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available","speed":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b0a4c65e-9f3e-43cb-94ec-f92dfc6ccb07","uuid":"7cbccf60-2995-49c5-89b0-f100da893ffb"}
